925 entries, decision-synthesizer to deepscientist-optimize. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **decision-tracker**: Use this agent to record, query, and analyze decisions from the Cortex decision journal. It tracks architectural choices, technology selections, user preferences, and their outcomes (success/failure/pending). &lt;example&gt; Context: User makes a technology decision user: "Let's use Django + HTMX for this project, not React" assistant: "I'll record this decision and use the decision-tracker to log it." &lt;commentary&gt; Technology selection is a trackable decision. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to review past decisions user: "What decisions have we made for this project?" assist… ([PretInnov-Inc/Claude-Plugin-Marketplace](https://github.com/PretInnov-Inc/Claude-Plugin-Marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cortex/agents/decision-tracker.md) · ★1)
- **decision-tracker**: Specialized agent that identifies and records key architectural and engineering decisions during coding sessions. Identifies architectural decisions during code changes, records technology choices with rationale and alternatives considered, captures schema changes, API contract modifications, and infrastructure decisions, adds structured context to TraceMem decision envelopes, and uses one-shot decision recording when no envelope is active. ([tracemem/tracemem-claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/tracemem/tracemem-claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-tracker.md) · ★1)
- **decision-tree**: Visual decision tree to help developers quickly select the right AI agent (Gemini CLI, Claude Code, Codex) for any task. ([FreakTwisted666/Nexus-02](https://github.com/FreakTwisted666/Nexus-02/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decision-tree.md))
- **decision-under-uncertainty**: Quantitative decision analyst. Defines the decision and options, establishes a reference-class base rate, updates with capped likelihood ratios while de-duplicating correlated evidence, evaluates failure chains with leave-one-out criticality, ranks options by expected value or utility, models attempts under a budget, and returns a Go/No-go/Conditional verdict with pre-registered thresholds and a stop rule. ([lxj2066/decision-under-uncertainty](https://github.com/lxj2066/decision-under-uncertainty/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-under-uncertainty.md) · ★1)
- **decision-watcher**: Standing periodic-review seat for one repo's ratified ADRs — detects (via a checkpointed content-hash diff) which ADR Decisions are new/amended since the last firing and which ADRs were just superseded, judges each against `save-lessons`'s frequency/impact bar scoped to that file, and queues candidates durably instead of blocking on a live human. Also runs a Revalidation mode (idr-0009): a sampled, round-robin RE-TEST of already-accepted ADR Decisions, locked IDR falsification clauses, and locked RDD Acceptance sections against present-day reality, tri-state verdict (confirmed/falsified/untes… ([kimgranlund/claude-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/decision-watcher.md))

- **decisions**: What the owner decided. An agent does not revisit a decision: a change needs the owner's word, and a departure found in the code is a defect, not an option. ([kvokka/loop-manager](https://github.com/kvokka/loop-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decisions.md))

- **decisions-correlator**: Given a scope prose + mode, globs docs/decisions/*.md, reads only the frontmatter of each candidate, applies a per-mode candidate-pool filter, and returns a ranked shortlist of decisions docs semantically correlated with the scope. Used by plan-context in both phase mode (pool = ad-hoc docs with `related_phases: []`) and task mode (pool = all docs except the task's own) to surface potentially relevant prior decisions without loading their bodies. The main thread asks the user to confirm the final set via AskUserQuestion. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-correlator.md) · ★28 · 11 copies)
- **decisions-detail-reader**: Reads the decisions documents tied to a target phase or task and extracts, per decided TD, only the `**Recommendation:**` prose + `**Libraries:**` line. Returns a compact `## Decisions Detail for Phase NN` / `## Decisions Detail for Task {slug}` block ready for direct embedding in context.md. Use when a stage of the plan pipeline needs the per-TD prose that `/plan-build` consumes when drafting Technical actions, without loading full TD bodies into the main thread. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-detail-reader.md) · ★28 · 11 copies)
- **decisions-format**: **Rule (owner, 2026-10-09):** if a PR or ticket needs the owner to choose something, it gives him the options to answer, and it can't be approved until the required ones are answered. Asking in prose ("A, B or C?", a "Choose" or "Open questions" heading, a checklist item ending in "?") is **too vague**: the dashboard shows *Too vague: needs options*, hides Approve, and the merge server refuses it ([G-Eskayo/marvin](https://github.com/G-Eskayo/marvin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decisions-format.md) · ★3)
- **decisions-generator**: Phase-2 macro-agent. Thin wrapper that preloads the `decisions-generator` skill and runs it in an isolated context with tool whitelisting. Generates stress-tested ADRs via divergent candidates + adversarial pairwise comparison. Use proactively when Phase-1 artefacts exist and ADRs must be drafted, stress-tested, or accepted — triggers: "generate decisions", "propose ADRs", "run Phase 2", "evaluate options", "accept the flow-variant decision", "stress-test this decision". ([rd162/OSS_Migration](https://github.com/rd162/OSS_Migration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-generator.md))

- **deeppattern-discipline-honesty**: Sources (public MIT, 2026-09): ([IgorGanapolsky/ThumbGate](https://github.com/IgorGanapolsky/ThumbGate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/deeppattern-discipline-honesty.md))
- **deepplan-architect**: Deep multi-wave planner for complex brownfield initiatives — decomposes objectives into dependency-grouped waves with per-wave synthesis gates ([krzemienski/shannon](https://github.com/krzemienski/shannon/blob/HEAD/agents/deepplan-architect.md) · ★2)
- **DeepResearch.agent**: Specialized Research & Analysis Agent – Use this for deep-dive investigations, comparisons, and audits without modifying code. ([GodSpeedAI/DomainForge](https://github.com/GodSpeedAI/DomainForge/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/DeepResearch.agent.md) · ★3)
- **DeepResearcher**: Deep researcher for comprehensive investigations. Called BY Research skill workflows only. Excels at multi-query decomposition, parallel search execution, and synthesizing scholarly sources. ([johansenmeister/Dual-PAI](https://github.com/johansenmeister/Dual-PAI/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/agents/DeepResearcher.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **DeepResearcherContext**: ../../../.opencode/skills/Agents/DeepResearcherContext.md ([johansenmeister/Dual-PAI](https://github.com/johansenmeister/Dual-PAI/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/agents/DeepResearcherContext.md) · ★2)
- **DeepScientist Core Agent**: Core operating contract shared by all DeepScientist quest turns. ([ResearAI/DeepScientist](https://github.com/ResearAI/DeepScientist/blob/HEAD/assets/text/agents/core-agent.md) · ★3.4k · 6 copies)
- **DeepScientist Worker Base**: Shared execution contract for specialized DeepScientist workers. ([ResearAI/DeepScientist](https://github.com/ResearAI/DeepScientist/blob/HEAD/assets/text/agents/worker-base.md) · ★3.4k · 6 copies)
- **deepscientist-analysis-campaign**: Use when a quest needs one or more follow-up runs such as ablations, robustness checks, error analysis, or failure analysis after a main experiment. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-analysis-campaign.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-baseline**: Use when a quest needs to attach, import, reproduce, repair, verify, compare, or publish a baseline and its metrics. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-baseline.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-decision**: Use when the quest needs an explicit go, stop, branch, reuse-baseline, write, finalize, reset, or user-decision transition with reasons and evidence. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-decision.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-experiment**: Use when a quest is ready for a concrete implementation pass or a main experiment run tied to a selected idea and an accepted baseline. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-experiment.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-figure-polish**: Use when a quest needs a polished milestone chart, paper-facing figure, appendix figure, or a mandatory render-inspect-revise pass before treating a figure as final. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-figure-polish.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-finalize**: Use when the quest is ready to consolidate final claims, limitations, recommendations, summary state, and graph exports before stopping or archiving. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-finalize.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-idea**: Use when a quest needs concrete hypotheses, limitation analysis, candidate directions, or a selected idea relative to the active baseline. - **deepscientist-intake-audit**: Use when a quest does not start from a blank state and the agent must first audit, trust-rank, and reconcile existing baselines, results, drafts, or review materials before choosing the next anchor. - **deepscientist-mentor**: Use when the work needs founder-level calibration for architecture convergence, verification rigor, product or UI taste, or when the user explicitly asks for mentor-style guidance aligned with the repository owner's standards. - **deepscientist-optimize**: Use when an algorithm-first quest should manage candidate briefs, optimization frontier, branch promotion, or fusion-aware search instead of the paper-oriented default loop.
